Storytel

http://www.purl.org/storytel/
Creator
Giorgia Crosilla
Description
Storytel is the ontology of the european ERASMUS+ project Storytel. Its goal is to map the relationship between Full Video, Story and Anonymized Video re-using existing ontologies and implementing some ad-hoc classes.
Language
English
Title
Storytel
imports
http://cidoc-crm.org/extensions/lrmoo/owl/1.0/
http://www.cidoc-crm.org/cidoc-crm/owl/7.1.3/
http://www.cidoc-crm.org/extensions/crmdig/owl/5.0/
Other visualisation
WebVOWL Source
Export
Turtle RDF/XML JSON-LD N-Triples

Age of the Participants
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.purl.org/storytel/Age_Of_The_Participants

comment
Dimension expressing the age of the participants in a group, expressed as an integer value between 18 and 65.
en
is defined by
Storytel
is sub concept of
p90 has valueop
some <= restricted by 65
e54 dimensionc
label
Age of the Participants
en
Title
Age of the Participants
en

@prefix : <http://www.purl.org/storytel/> .
@prefix cidoc-crm: <http://www.cidoc-crm.org/cidoc-crm/> .
@prefix dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/> .
@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

:Age_Of_The_Participants a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "Age of the Participants"@en ;
    dc:title "Age of the Participants"@en ;
    rdfs:comment "Dimension expressing the age of the participants in a group, expressed as an integer value between 18 and 65."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy : ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty cidoc-crm:P90_has_value ;
            owl:someValuesFrom [ a rdfs:Datatype ;
                    owl:onDatatype xsd:integer ;
                    owl:withRestrictions ( [ xsd:minInclusive 18 ] [ xsd:maxInclusive 65 ] ) ] ],
        cidoc-crm:E54_Dimension .

Annotation
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.purl.org/storytel/Annotation

comment
Annotation object attached to a segment of an Anonymized Story, whose body carries the annotation content such as a transcription, translation, glossing etc. It is created using the Storytel AI pipeline
en
is defined by
Storytel
is sub concept of
l43 annotatesop
some
Anonymized Storyc
has bodyop
some
Annotation Bodyc
has targetop
some
Segment of Anonymized Storyc
d29 annotation objectc,
Annotationc
label
Annotation
en
Title
Annotation
en

@prefix : <http://www.purl.org/storytel/> .
@prefix crmdig: <http://www.cidoc-crm.org/extensions/crmdig/> .
@prefix dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/> .
@prefix oa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/oa#> .
@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .

:Annotation a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "Annotation"@en ;
    dc:title "Annotation "@en ;
    rdfs:comment "Annotation object attached to a segment of an Anonymized Story, whose body carries the annotation content such as a transcription, translation, glossing etc. It is created using the Storytel AI pipeline"@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy : ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty crmdig:L43_annotates ;
            owl:someValuesFrom :Anonymized_Story ],
        [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty oa:hasBody ;
            owl:someValuesFrom :Annotation_Body ],
        [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty oa:hasTarget ;
            owl:someValuesFrom :Anonymized_Story_Segment ],
        crmdig:D29_Annotation_Object,
        oa:Annotation .
